11                               ,Order for Extraction mode
28                               ,Starting order for Analysis mode
31                               ,Ending order for Analysis mode
1                                 ,Index of one of about 20 generator methods
1                                  ,Index of database types, [1,2,3,4] => [ALL,Essential,Symm,G-Symm]
4                                  ,[1,2,3,4] filter on number of ones in DAF rectangle [NONE,3X3,5X5,User Defined]
100                             ,Maximum number of ones in specified DAF area
rows -2 to +2, cols -2 to +2    ,Input string for rows and columns defining rectangle in DAF
1                                  ,Index of frequency hop methods [NONE,Min,Min end-around]
F                                  ,.TRUE. => output CSV files with DAFs
F                                  ,.TRUE. => output CSV files with XDAFs
F                                  ,.TRUE. => find generating polynomial in GF(q)
0                               ,Order q of GF(q) for generating polynomial
F                                  ,.TRUE. => Write analysis of entire database (not sticky)
F                                  ,.TRUE. => Write counts in 3X3 central areas of DAFs (not sticky)
F                                  ,.TRUE. => Write counts in 5X5 central ares of DAFs (not sticky)
F                                  ,.TRUE. => Recompute minimum peak freuency hop database (not sticky)
